# Cloud & AI Infrastructure Solution Architect Complete Bilingual Catalog (564 Deliverables · `#001–#562`)

> **Language / 语言**: [简体中文](./full-catalog-zh.md) | **English** · [10-Layer Stack Map](./stack-map.en.md) · [SA Competency Roadmap](./learning/roadmap.en.md) · [🎓 AI Infra H5 Panorama](./h5/index.html) · [🔥 Interactive H5 Learning Hub](./h5/ai-infra-learning-hub.en.html) · [Repository Home](../README.en.md)

> **Overview**: This master catalog indexes all **564 bilingual (`zh-CN` & `en`) Cloud & AI Infrastructure Solution Architect deliverables** in this repository (**2 Flagship Full-Stack Reference Architectures** + **562 Component Selection & Enterprise Architecture FAQs `#001–#562`**). Every guide is structured with **Executive Summary, Real-World Scenario, Architecture Trade-offs, Quantitative Decision Matrix, and Pre-Production Checklist**, paired with self-contained **Interactive H5 Study Labs (`docs/h5/`)**, **HTML Sizers**, **7-Card 4:5 Visual Decks**, and **Reproducible Python Models**.
